[Generative_AI_LLM_Report v1.pdf](https://github.com/user-attachments/files/27096822/Generative_AI_LLM_Report.v1.pdf)# EXP-1-PROMPT-ENGINEERING-

## Aim: 
Comprehensive Report on the Fundamentals of Generative AI and Large Language Models (LLMs)
Experiment: Develop a comprehensive report for the following exercises:

Explain the foundational concepts of Generative AI.
Focusing on Generative AI architectures. (like transformers).
Generative AI applications.
Generative AI impact of scaling in LLMs.

## Algorithm:
Generate a comprehensive, professional report PDF on the fundamentals of Generative AI and Large Language Models (LLMs). Develop a detailed report covering the following topics: Explain the foundational concepts of Generative AI, with a focus on Generative AI architectures (such as transformers), Generative AI applications, and the impact of scaling in LLMs. Ensure the report is neat, clean, and professional. Add appropriate images or flowcharts to make each concept clear and easy to understand, and act as a Senior AI Researcher and Technical Writer.
